To bridge local continuous spatial geometry with this discrete algebraic backend without requiring power-intensive online training loops, we introduce a decoupled, hybrid neuro-symbolic control pipeline. Local multi-layered neural networks operate as structural coordination encoders, pre-trained offline to nonlinearly map hand-engineered geometric descriptors into abstract environmental target measures. At runtime, edge agents execute instantaneous behavioral adaptations by applying the Radon-Nikodým derivative directly as a zero-shot importance-reweighting filter. We validate the framework within a discrete-time multi-agent simulation sandbox spanning tracking, decentralized dispersion/area coverage, and multi-goal coordination regimes. Qualitative telemetry demonstrates that the integrated pipeline achieves stable target-directed motion, anti-collapse spatial spreading under decentralized constraints, and structured subgroup formation across multiple targets, providing a mathematically grounded route to tractable, low-power edge swarm intelligence.

</details>


### [112] [Boundary Variance Inflation Causes Acquisition Bias in Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07561)
*Maria Bånkestad,Sanna Jarl,Jens Sjölund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 高斯过程在边界域的边界处后验方差膨胀，导致采集偏差；本文揭示其几何机制并引入无函数选择的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管边界引起的后验方差膨胀是长期被识别的伪影（地质统计学）和过度探索源（贝叶斯优化），但其成因和影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 追踪到核相关邻域在域边界截断这一简单几何机制；通过三类采集函数（方差最大化、负积分后验方差、期望预测信息增益）展示边界扭曲；提出无函数的采集选择剖面诊断方法。

Result: 方差最大化将选择集中在角落，而后两者将选择向内移向轴向内部壳层；采集行为可能被核几何主导而非任务相关的不确定性。

Conclusion: 边界诱导的采集偏差源于核相关邻域截断的几何机制，且随维度增加而恶化；提出的诊断方法适用于任意采集函数、核和边界域几何。

Abstract: Gaussian processes with stationary kernels on bounded domains exhibit inflated posterior variance near the boundary. Despite being a long-recognized artifact in geostatistics and a source of over-exploration in Bayesian optimization, the causes and effects of boundary-induced acquisition bias are underexplored. We trace the root cause to a simple geometric mechanism: the truncation of the kernel correlation neighborhood at the domain boundary creates an observation-independent distortion that worsens with dimensionality. We show how this distortion manifests across three acquisition classes: variance maximization concentrates selections at the corners, whereas negative integrated posterior variance and expected predictive information gain move selections inward to axis-aligned interior shells. These patterns arise without reference to any objective function, meaning that acquisition behavior can be dominated by kernel geometry rather than the desired task-specific uncertainty. To quantify this, we introduce a function-free selection-profile diagnostic for arbitrary acquisitions, kernels, and bounded-domain geometries.

</details>


### [113] [Emergence via Phase Transitions: Mechanism Landscapes and Universal Convergence Across Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07563)
*Truong Xuan Khanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层级涌现框架（HEF），将涌现建模为机制景观中的相变，受热力学和信息论约束。在111个模运算Transformer实验中发现，权重范数在92%的运行中在grokking前系统性地达到峰值，准确率曲线塌缩到tanh扭结（R²=0.93），所有grokked模型收敛到0.9745±0.014，与初始条件无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习、生物学和物理学中独立演化的系统往往趋同于相似的高层结构（如grokking电路、代谢解决方案、重整化流），但缺乏统一的机制解释框架。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Emergence Framework (HEF)，引入临界能量阈值Ec将机制景观划分为探索区和收敛区；证明度规收缩和唯一不动点收敛；通过有效信息和机制竞争熵连接因果涌现。

Result: 111个实验中92%的run在grokking前权重范数系统性地达到峰值；归一化准确率曲线塌缩到tanh扭结（R²=0.93），符合Landau-Ginzburg普适类；所有grokked模型收敛到0.9745±0.014（ANOVA p>0.13），不受初始化、权重衰减或训练比例影响。

Conclusion: HEF不是一个通用的涌现理论，而是一个可证伪的数学框架，用于研究跨复杂系统的收敛现象。

Abstract: Across machine learning, biology, and physics, independently evolving systems often converge toward strikingly similar high-level structures despite radically different microscopic details. Grokking circuits converge across random seeds, evolutionary lineages rediscover similar metabolic solutions, and renormalization flows approach common fixed points. We propose the Hierarchical Emergence Framework (HEF) as a candidate universality framework for such convergence phenomena. HEF models emergence as a phase transition in a mechanism landscape constrained by thermodynamic and information-theoretic laws. The framework introduces a critical energy threshold Ec separating an exploration regime with competing mechanisms from a convergence regime governed by a unique minimum-cost mechanism. Under structural assumptions, we prove physical feasibility, derive strict metric contraction, and establish convergence toward a unique fixed-point representation independent of initial conditions. We further connect this convergence structure to causal emergence through Effective Information and mechanism competition entropy. To test the framework, we study delayed generalization ("grokking") in modular arithmetic transformers across 111 experiments. We identify a reproducible empirical fingerprint of the Ec transition: the weight norm peaks systematically before grokking in 92% of runs. Normalized accuracy curves collapse onto a tanh kink (R^2=0.93) consistent with a Landau-Ginzburg universality class, and all grokked models converge to 0.9745+/-0.014 regardless of initialization, weight decay, or training fraction (ANOVA p>0.13). HEF is not presented as a universal theory of emergence, but as a falsifiable mathematical scaffold for studying convergence phenomena across complex systems.

</details>


### [114] [STARIXNet: Multivariate and Multi-attribute Deep Learning Approach to Real-Time Resource Allocation in Cloud Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07565)
*Ahmed Abdulaal,Maruf Aytekin,Thilaga kumaran Srinivasan,Tomer Lancewicki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STARIXNet是一个轻量级神经网络，通过捕捉多系统指标间的时空关系来指导微服务资源分配决策，优先保障服务稳定性而非纯粹的预测精度，在沃尔玛实际部署中实现10%-50%的成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微服务扩缩容方案局限于单一指标（如CPU使用率）的预测，忽略低估风险与响应延迟，或计算复杂度过高难以实时部署。亟需一种轻量级、多变量的智能扩缩容方案。

Method: STARIXNet建模季节性、时序、自回归集成与外生模式等多变量准依赖属性，捕捉系统指标间的时空关系，然后通过聚合策略做出扩缩容决策，优先保障服务稳定性，其次考虑成本效率，最后才是预测精度。

Result: 在真实环境中与现有方案对比，STARIXNet在沃尔玛关键生产微服务中实现10%-50%的显著成本节约，同时提升服务稳定性和客户体验。

Conclusion: STARIXNet通过多变量时空建模与优先级排序的聚合策略，有效平衡了成本效率与服务稳定性，是一种适用于大规模实时部署的轻量级微服务扩缩容解决方案。

Abstract: Intelligent scaling of microservices in cloud platforms is crucial for mitigating escalating compute costs while avoiding service disruptions. Current solutions are limited to the univariate space, typically focusing on CPU usage alone to drive scaling decisions. Moreover, they address the problem as a purely forecasting task, focusing on prediction precision while neglecting the greater risks of underestimation and delays in system responsiveness. Alternative solutions are computationally complex, making them impractical for large-scale, real-time deployments. To address these challenges, we present STARIXNet, a lightweight neural network that guides resource allocation decisions in the multivariate space by capturing spatio-temporal relationships among multiple system metrics. STARIXNet models multiple quasi-dependent attributes, in particular the (S)easonal, (T)emporal, (A)uto-(R)egressive (I)ntegrated, and e(X)ogenous patterns, then implements an aggregation policy to finalize scaling decisions, prioritizing service stability, followed by cost-efficiency, over raw forecast accuracy. We empirically demonstrate the performance of STARIXNet by benchmarking against existing solutions in real-world settings. STARIXNet is deployed for critical production microservices at Walmart achieving tangible savings ranging from 10\% to 50\%, in addition to intangible benefits through improved service stability and customer experience.

</details>


### [115] [TriHead-GAN: A Generative Adversarial Network with Triple-Head Discriminator for Carbon Emission Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07569)
*Zesen Wang,Lijuan Lan,Yonggang Li,Chunhua Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TriHead-GAN，一种基于Transformer的对抗生成框架，通过三头判别器联合监督碳排放时间序列的分布真实性、跨变量依赖性和步态平滑性，在多个数据集上优于主流方法并提升下游预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级碳排放高频监测数据极度匮乏，限制了深度学习模型的应用；现有生成模型（GAN、扩散模型）难以保留碳排放数据中的跨变量相关性和步态变异性。

Method: 提出TriHead-GAN，生成器采用全局自注意力+局部时序卷积+逐步噪声注入+抗平滑损失；判别器为三头结构：Wasserstein评论家（分布真实性）、泄漏无关回归（跨变量依赖）、相邻差分预测（步态平滑性）。

Result: 在自采集的长沙碳数据集、两个公开碳数据集（中国、美国）及ETTh1基准上，TriHead-GAN在绝大多数设置下优于主流基线，生成的合成样本可提升低资源碳监测场景的下游预测精度。

Conclusion: TriHead-GAN通过多维度联合监督有效解决了碳排放时间序列生成中跨变量依赖和步态变异性的保持问题，验证了合成数据增强在碳监测中的实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate carbon emission monitoring is critical for climate policy and emerging regulatory mechanisms such as the EU Carbon Border Adjustment Mechanism, yet city-level high-frequency monitoring data remain extremely scarce, severely limiting data-hungry deep learning models. Time series generation is a natural remedy, but existing GAN and diffusion-based generators often provide limited explicit supervision for the domain structure of carbon emission data: they may match marginal distributional statistics while insufficiently preserving cross-variable correlations between CO$_2$ and co-emitted pollutants and meteorological factors, and tend to collapse the first-difference statistics of atmospheric measurements, producing sequences that are smooth on average but lack the realistic step-wise variability of the underlying signals. We propose TriHead-GAN, a Transformer-based adversarial framework whose triple-head discriminator jointly supervises three complementary aspects of the joint distribution: distributional authenticity via a Wasserstein critic, cross-variable dependency via leakage-free regression of the target variable, and step-wise temporal smoothness via adjacent-difference prediction. The generator combines global self-attention with local temporal convolution, per-step noise injection, and an anti-smoothing loss that matches first-difference statistics. Experiments on the self-collected Changsha Carbon dataset, two public carbon datasets (China, US), and the ETTh1 benchmark show that TriHead-GAN achieves favorable performance over mainstream baselines on the vast majority of settings, and that the resulting synthetic windows improve downstream forecasting accuracy in low-resource carbon monitoring scenarios.

</details>


### [116] [Enabling KV Caching of Shared Prefix for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07571)
*Younghun Go,Jaehoon Han,Changyong Shin,Chuk Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出bicache，首个针对扩散语言模型(DLM)的双向前缀缓存技术，通过动态识别安全层深来复用共享前缀KV缓存，避免LLM缓存技术直接应用导致的精度崩溃问题，实现36.3%-98.3%的吞吐量提升且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的KV缓存技术假设KVs计算后不变，但DLM的双向注意力机制中更新任意token会动态改变整个上下文及其KVs，直接套用会导致模型精度几乎归零。因此需要专门为DLM设计高效的前缀KV缓存技术以支持高吞吐服务。

Method: 通过全面分析发现共享前缀KVs在浅层保持稳定可重用，且浅层深度取决于每个请求中共享前缀token的比例。基于此观察，bicache动态识别可安全重用共享前缀KV的层深度，消除冗余计算。

Result: 相比现有技术，bicache将服务吞吐量提升36.3%-98.3%，且精度几乎无崩溃（仅有0-1.8%的差异）。

Conclusion: bicache有效解决了DLM中共享前缀KV缓存的难题，为扩散语言模型的高吞吐服务提供了可行的缓存方案。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching for shared prefixes is essential for high-throughput large language model (LLM) serving, but it faces critical challenges in emerging diffusion language models (DLMs). In DLMs, bidirectional attention means that updating any token dynamically alters the entire context and its corresponding KVs. Thus, existing caching techniques developed for LLMs, which assume that KVs remain invariant once computed, corrupt the shared prefix KVs. Our experiments show that applying these techniques to DLMs causes model accuracy to collapse to near zero.
  To unlock high-throughput DLM serving, we propose bidirectional prefix caching, bicache, the first KV caching technique for shared prefixes in DLMs. bicache is designed based on key observations from our comprehensive analysis: shared prefix KVs remain stable and reusable in shallow layers, while the depth of shallow layers depends on the fraction of shared prefix tokens in each request. Thus, bicache dynamically identifies a safe layer depth for reusing shared prefix KVs and eliminates redundant computation. Evaluations demonstrate that bicache significantly improves serving throughput by 36.3%-98.3% compared to existing techniques without accuracy collapse (only 0-1.8% difference).

</details>


### [117] [When Should an AI Scientist Stop? Verifiable Experiment Steering and Refusal for Autonomous Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07576)
*Neel Tushar Shah,Manglam Kartik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARTOGRAPH是一个用于AI科学家的验证层，通过选择（select）、解析（resolve）和拒绝（refuse）三个机制来提升实验验证的可靠性。实验表明，该方法在多个测试平台上优于基线方法，并能有效识别不可靠的科学论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学家在自主实验设计中存在验证不足的问题，传统方法无法有效处理未解析子空间、模糊闭合和库不匹配等情况，需要更可靠的验证框架。

Method: 提出CARTOGRAPH验证层，包含三个核心模块：(1) select：未解析子空间实验导向；(2) resolve：显式模糊闭合；(3) refuse：基于残差的库不充分性检测。在局部线性-高斯桥下推导了未解析投影的Fisher信息量，并提出了CARTOGRAPH-A精确A-最优规则。

Result: 在五个测试平台上，CARTOGRAPH-A在d=8时以129胜/0平/15负显著优于基线方法（p<10^-21）；在药代动力学测试中识别出三个库外机制后因残差暴露结构失配而撤销；在EPA低维设置中观察到与理论预测一致的结果；在A-Lab材料系统的40个阳性论断审计中，拒止模块标记了所有4个后来被人工复核标记为不确定的论断，同时通过了32/36个确认断言。

Conclusion: CARTOGRAPH为AI科学家提供了一个有效的验证层，能够显著提升实验设计的可靠性，特别是在识别虚假科学论断方面表现出色。

Abstract: We present CARTOGRAPH, a verification layer for AI scientists that couples unresolved-subspace experiment steering (select), explicit ambiguity closure (resolve), and residual-based library inadequacy detection (refuse). Under a local linear-Gaussian bridge, raw unresolved projection is the isotropic unresolved Fisher-information trace, while CARTOGRAPH-A is the exact unresolved A-optimal rule; closed-form EIG and Box-Hill arise as local comparators rather than global equivalents. Across five testbeds, CARTOGRAPH-A beats raw projection 129W/0T/15L at d = 8 (p < 10^-21) in a replicated structured cascade. More distinctively, the framework tentatively identifies three out-of-library pharmacokinetic mechanisms and then revokes those identifications as residuals expose structural misfit, while one perturbed in-library control stays identified throughout. In low-dimensional pharmacokinetic and filtered EPA settings, near-ties against disagreement are predicted by theory and observed. Finally, in a retrospective audit of 40 positive claims from the published A-Lab autonomous materials system, the refuse guard flags all 4 claims later marked inconclusive under manual reanalysis while passing 32/36 confirmed claims. Code is available at https://github.com/ai4science-boed/cartograph.git

</details>


### [118] [MST-Direct at Scale: Multivariate and Conditional Geostatistical Simulation via Sinkhorn Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07578)
*Tcharlies Bachmann Schmitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MST-Direct方法，将其从原始的双变量、无条件、小网格形式推广到多变量、条件化、大网格场景，通过稀疏Sinkhorn匹配器、FFT-MA高斯骨架和克里金条件化，实现了精确的多元地质统计模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始MST-Direct方法存在三个主要局限性：难以扩展到数千节点以上的大网格、仅支持双变量、无法进行硬数据条件化。本文旨在解决这些问题，实现更通用的多元地质统计模拟。

Method: (i) 提出稀疏、候选受限的Sinkhorn匹配器，具有O(nC)内存复杂度，提升可扩展性；(ii) 通过将目标值元组匹配到独立的FFT-MA高斯骨架上，扩展至多变量；(iii) 通过克里金对骨架进行条件化，并在空间位置上固定观测数据元组来实现硬数据条件化。

Result: 在六变量异方差强非线性参考分布上验证，无条件和条件场景下均表现优异：零直方图误差、精确还原硬数据、准确重现预设空间相关结构。而PPMT方法仅为近似。

Conclusion: MST-Direct方法在多元地质统计模拟中显著优于PPMT，能够精确保持多元联合分布，为实际地质统计学应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper extends MST-Direct, a Matching-via-Sinkhorn-Transport approach for multivariate geostatistical simulation, from the original bivariate, unconditional, small-grid formulation to multivariate, conditional, and large-grid settings. We address the three main limitations identified in the original work: (i) scalability beyond a few thousand nodes through a sparse, candidate-restricted Sinkhorn matcher with O(nC) memory complexity; (ii) extension to multiple variables by matching target value tuples onto an independent FFT-MA Gaussian backbone that reproduces a prescribed variogram; and (iii) hard-data conditioning by fixing observed data tuples at their spatial locations while conditioning the backbone through kriging. Because the transport plan remains a permutation of the target tuples, the multivariate joint distribution is preserved exactly.
  The method is validated using the same six-variate, heteroscedastic, strongly nonlinear reference distribution employed in Direct Multivariate Simulation (DMS), under both unconditional (200x200) and conditional (100x100, 200 hard-data samples) scenarios, and is benchmarked against the Projection Pursuit Multivariate Transform (PPMT). Results show that MST-Direct reproduces the joint distribution with zero histogram error, exactly honours hard data, and accurately reproduces the prescribed spatial correlation structure, whereas PPMT remains an approximation.
  Index Terms-Optimal transport, Sinkhorn algorithm, geostatistical simulation, multivariate simulation.

</details>


### [119] [Training-Inference Kernel Contracts: Bounding Divergence in Post-Training and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07581)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Lan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"内核契约"框架，用于规范训练与推理内核在有限精度下的行为差异，通过数值、统计、运行时和可观测性条款，以及对策略梯度偏差的理论界限，解决后训练管线中的符号不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代后训练管线中，策略π_θ使用单一符号表示，但训练内核（优化自动求导）与推理内核（优化低精度、融合、动态批处理服务）在有限精度下可能产生不同分布，且差异集中在基准测试未充分覆盖的切片上，导致评估与训练不一致。

Method: 提出内核契约框架，包含数值(N)、统计(S)、运行时(R)、可观测性(O)条款及升级策略(Pi)；推导从logit漂移到TV距离再到有界奖励漂移的界限链；针对RL后训练，在显式支持和范数假设下，通过每token重要性比率漂移约束策略梯度偏差；设计四阶段晋升管线、在线路由循环和YAML DSL契约工件。

Result: 构建了完整的理论界限链，将logit级偏差与策略梯度偏差联系起来；设计了可落地的四阶段晋升管线与在线路由机制；但本文为框架与词汇论文，未提供生产规模实证验证。

Conclusion: 内核契约为训练-推理符号不一致问题提供了系统化的解决方案框架，为未来在不同精度内核间建立可量化的行为契约奠定了基础。

Abstract: A modern post-training pipeline often writes one symbol for its policy, pi_theta, while evaluating it through two different programs: a training kernel optimized for autograd and an inference kernel optimized for low-precision, fused, dynamically batched serving. In finite precision, these kernels can induce different distributions at identical weights, with the gap concentrated on slices that aggregate benchmarks under-represent. This paper proposes kernel contracts: a contract-first framework for specifying acceptable divergence between K_train and K_inf. A contract C = (N, S, R, O, Pi) combines numerical, statistical, runtime, and observability clauses with an escalation policy from violations to routing actions. We derive a chain of bounds from logit drift to total-variation distance to bounded reward drift, and specialize it to RL post-training, where per-token importance-ratio drift yields a bound on policy-gradient bias under explicit support and norm assumptions. We also describe a four-stage promotion pipeline, online routing loop, and minimal YAML DSL for contract artifacts. This is a framework and vocabulary paper; we do not report production-scale empirical validation.

</details>


### [120] [Customer Churn Prediction on Structured Data Using FT-Transformer and Stacking Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07582)
*Joyjit Roy,Samaresh Kumar Singh,Laxmi Shaw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合FT-Transformer与XGBoost的混合架构，通过校准感知堆叠（calibration-aware stacking）进行客户流失预测，在公开银行流失数据集上取得了优于MLP基线的性能（F1 62.10%，AUC-ROC 0.861），并通过消融实验验证了各模块的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保险、数字银行、电商等数据驱动行业中，客户流失预测至关重要，但面临类别不平衡、非线性特征交互和异构特征类型等挑战。现有研究在统计验证、概率校准和可重复性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出混合架构，将特征分词变换器（FT-Transformer）与XGBoost通过校准感知堆叠集成。FT-Transformer利用自注意力机制捕获高阶特征交互，XGBoost利用梯度提升捕获决策边界。使用类别加权损失函数处理类别不平衡，避免合成过采样。通过基于逻辑回归元学习器的OOF堆叠进行集成，重新校准过于自信的基模型输出。

Result: 在公开银行流失数据集上，混合模型在5×5交叉验证下达到F1 62.10%、AUC-ROC 0.861、PR-AUC 0.647，相比MLP基线提升3.37 F1点和0.027 AUC，并报告了95%置信区间。

Conclusion: 该混合架构是一种可重复、可扩展的参考方案，适用于结构化表格数据的客户流失预测场景。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction is essential across data-driven industries such as insurance, digital banking, eCommerce, and subscription platforms, where retaining existing customers is typically more cost-effective than acquiring new ones. Predicting churn on structured datasets remains challenging due to class imbalance, nonlinear feature interactions, and heterogeneous feature types. Tree-based ensemble methods consistently demonstrate strong performance in these contexts, often outperforming conventional neural networks. This study introduces a validated hybrid architecture that integrates feature-tokenized transformers (FT-Transformer) with gradient-boosted trees through calibration-aware stacking. The proposed framework addresses persistent gaps in statistical validation, probability calibration, and reproducibility found in prior research. The FT-Transformer captures higher-order feature interactions using self-attention, while XGBoost captures gradient-boosted decision boundaries with complementary inductive biases. Class imbalance is handled using class-weighted loss functions, thereby avoiding synthetic oversampling and preserving minority-class distributions. The models are ensembled using out-of-fold (OOF) stacking with a logistic regression meta-learner, which recalibrates overconfident base model outputs and learns optimal combination weights. On a public bank churn dataset, the hybrid model achieves 62.10% F1, 0.861 AUC-ROC, and 0.647 PR-AUC, outperforming the Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) baseline by 3.37 F1 points and 0.027 AUC under 5x5 cross-validation with 95% confidence intervals reported. Ablation studies demonstrate that both the transformer component and stacking strategy contribute materially to performance. The proposed methodology offers a reproducible and extensible reference architecture for contemporary churn prediction on structured tabular data.

</details>


### [121] [Outage Detection in Self-Healing Smart Grids Using Reinforcement Learning with Spectral Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07583)
*Lihui Liu,Mucun Sun,Caisheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于谱图强化学习的配电网络故障管理框架，利用谱图神经网络学习最优电力恢复策略，在IEEE测试系统上实现了接近最优的实时性能和良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法响应速度慢、计算成本高，不适用于智能电网的故障缓解；而现有基于图神经网络的方法在空间域操作，无法捕捉频域中重要的全局结构模式和系统范围交互信息。

Method: 提出谱图强化学习框架，使用谱图神经网络（Spectral GNN）学习最优电力恢复策略，用于配电网络的故障管理。

Result: 在IEEE 13-bus、34-bus和123-bus三个改进测试系统上，该方法实现了接近最优的实时性能，并在多种故障场景下具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 谱图强化学习框架能有效利用频域信息，在实时性和泛化性方面优于传统方法，有助于提升智能电网的韧性。

Abstract: Self-healing smart grids can quickly adjust their network configuration during outages to minimize power disruptions. During an outage, several actions can be taken, such as network reconfiguration through switching operations and emergency load shedding. However, traditional machine learning methods for outage mitigation are not well suited for smart grids due to their slow response time and high computational cost. To address these challenges, recent studies have explored reinforcement learning to automatically perform network reconfiguration. In these approaches, the control policy is typically modeled using a graph neural network (GNN). However, conventional GNNs operate in the spatial domain and may fail to capture important relationships in the frequency domain. Frequency-domain information is particularly useful for modeling global structural patterns and system-wide interactions in power networks. In this paper, we propose a spectral graph reinforcement learning framework for outage management in distribution networks to enhance system resilience. Our model learns the optimal power restoration policy using a spectral graph neural network. We evaluate the proposed method on three modified IEEE test systems: the 13-bus, 34-bus, and 123-bus networks. Experimental results show that our approach achieves near-optimal performance in real time and generalizes well across a wide range of outage scenarios.

</details>


### [122] [From Human Guidance to Autonomy: Agent Skill System for End-to-End LLM Deployment on Spatial NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07586)
*Jiajie Li,Erwei Wang,Zhiru Zhang,Samuel Bayliss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法论，在AMD XDNA 2 NPU上实现了从人工引导到智能体自主的端到端LLM部署。第一阶段通过人工引导的智能体辅助部署Llama-3.2-1B，取得2.2倍（prefill）和4.0倍（decode）加速；第二阶段将经验蒸馏为智能体技能系统，自主部署了8个额外的解码器-only LLM，均通过数值正确性验证，部分模型性能达到或超过参考部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的空间NPU上进行端到端LLM部署仍然劳动密集，现有研究多聚焦于单核优化而非全局部署，缺乏自动化、可泛化的部署方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段，人工引导智能体辅助开发参考部署，记录优化轨迹；第二阶段，将文档蒸馏为包含八个阶段的智能体技能系统，在每个阶段严格实施数值正确性检查，自主部署多种LLM。

Result: Llama-3.2-1B实现2.2倍（prefill）和4.0倍（decode）加速；智能体系统自主部署8个LLM，每部署仅需0.5-4小时且几乎无需人工干预，均通过数值正确性验证；其中3个模型性能达到或超过参考部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体技能系统能够实现功能泛化至未见过的LLM，在降低人工成本的同时保持竞争性性能，为空间NPU上的端到端LLM自动化部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Spatial neural processing units (NPUs) provide an energy-efficient platform for edge LLM inference, but efficiently deploying an LLM end-to-end on such hardware remains labor-intensive. Although AI coding agents have begun to lower this cost, existing studies have largely focused on single-kernel optimization rather than end-to-end LLM deployment on resource-constrained spatial NPUs.
  We present a two-stage methodology, instantiated on the AMD XDNA 2 NPU, that progresses from human-guided development to agent autonomy. In the first stage, we develop a reference deployment of Llama-3.2-1B through human-guided agent assistance. The resulting implementation achieves a speedup of 2.2x on prefill and 4.0x on decode over the hand-optimized baseline, with the optimization trajectory and its lessons recorded as structured documentation throughout. In the second stage, we distill the documentation into an agent skill system consisting of eight phases, orchestrating the optimization and debugging skill sets, with numerical correctness strictly enforced at each phase.
  Using our agent skill system, we autonomously deploy eight additional decoder-only LLMs (Llama-3.2-3B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Qwen2.5-{0.5B, 1.5B, 3B}, Qwen3-{0.6B, 1.7B, 4B}) end-to-end on the AMD XDNA 2 NPU using the open-source compiler stack. To our knowledge, these models have not previously been deployed on AMD NPUs via any open-source software stack. Each deployment completes in 0.5-4 hours of agent wall time with almost no human guidance, and passes the numerical-correctness gates, demonstrating functional generalization to previously unencountered LLMs. Three of the eight match or exceed the sustained performance of our Llama-3.2-1B reference deployment, suggesting that the resulting implementations can be competitive without additional model-specific human engineering.

</details>


### [123] [The Routing Plateau: Understanding and Breaking the Accuracy Limits of LLM Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07587)
*Yifan Lu,Qiyue Zhang,Shenrun Zhang,Zhibo Yu,Zhuang Wang,Hanjie Chen,Jiarong Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对21种LLM路由方法进行系统研究，发现这些方法存在"路由高原"现象：不同方法性能趋同且远低于Oracle路由。究其原因，当前路由器主要学习全局平均性能趋势，而非细粒度的查询级路由信号。研究进一步揭示了突破高原的可行方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由通过动态选择模型来优化服务的成本-质量权衡，但现有路由方法的表现及其局限尚不明确，需要系统性的研究和分析。

Method: 对21种路由方法在5个基准上进行大规模对比研究，分析了聚类路由、分类器、成对排序、置信度方法等多种路由范式，并深入探究性能瓶颈的成因及突破路径。

Result: 发现了"路由高原"现象：不同路由方法性能收敛到狭窄范围，远低于Oracle路由器；瓶颈在于模型主要学习全局趋势而非查询级信号；所有方法在简单问题上表现相似，在需实例级路由的难题上集体失效。

Conclusion: 更大的训练集、更强的编码器和端到端微调可以突破路由高原，为构建更有效的路由系统提供了可操作的方向。

Abstract: LLM routing has become a popular approach to improve the cost-quality trade-off of LLM services by dynamically selecting a model for each query. Recent work has explored a broad range of routing methods, including clustering-based routers, learned classifiers, pairwise ranking, and confidence-based approaches. Our extensive study of 21 routing methods across five benchmarks reveals a consistent phenomenon that we call the routing plateau: many methods, including kNN, achieve very similar accuracy and converge to a narrow performance range that remains far below the oracle router. Our investigation shows that the plateau is largely caused by a predictability bottleneck: current routers mainly learn global averaged model-performance trends rather than fine-grained query-specific routing signals. As a result, they solve overlapping easy queries but collectively fail on hard queries that require instance-specific routing decisions. We further study how to move beyond the plateau and find that larger training datasets, stronger encoders, and end-to-end fine-tuning can further improve routing accuracy. These findings characterize the common limits of current routing methods and provide insights and actionable directions for the community to build more effective routing systems.

</details>


### [124] [Optimality of Sequential Filtering Under Independent Cost and Selectivity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07589)
*Hrishikesh Paranjape,Abhishek Mandal,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了顺序过滤管道问题，证明在独立性假设下，按成本与拒绝概率之比升序排列过滤器可最小化期望总成本，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证该最优排序策略在所有运行中均优于常见启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序过滤管道是大规模系统中的常见设计模式，但过滤器的排序通常依赖缺乏形式化保证的启发式方法，因此需要建立理论框架来指导最优排序。

Method: 形式化定义顺序过滤的期望成本目标，在独立性模型下推导最优排序条件（按成本/拒绝概率比升序），并通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟进行验证。

Result: 理论证明和蒙特卡洛模拟均表明，按成本与拒绝概率之比升序排列过滤器，在期望值和全分布结果上严格优于常见启发式排序方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为顺序过滤管道的最优排序提供了理论保证和实用指导，可广泛应用于排名系统、级联机器学习推理和欺诈检测等领域。

Abstract: Sequential filtering pipelines are a common design pattern in large-scale systems, where a large population of items is progressively reduced by a sequence of stages that each incur cost. Despite their prevalence in ranking systems, cascaded machine learning inference, and fraud detection, filter ordering is often determined by heuristics without formal guarantees. We formalize sequential filtering under an expected-cost objective and prove that, under an independence model, ordering filters by increasing ratio of cost to rejection probability minimizes expected total cost. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations show that the optimal ordering strictly dominates common heuristics across all runs, both in expectation and across the full distribution of outcomes.

</details>


### [125] [ResearchClawBench: A Benchmark for End-to-End Autonomous Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07591)
*Wanghan Xu,Shuo Li,Tianlin Ye,Qinglong Cao,Yixin Chen,Hengjian Gao,Yiheng Wang,Qi Li,Kun Li,Sheng Xu,Shengdu Chai,Fangchen Yu,Xiangyu Zhao,Zhangrui Zhao,Weijie Ma,Zijie Guo,Haoyu Zhou,Haoxiang Yin,Lixue Cheng,Chaofan Hu,Haoxuan Li,Lu Mi,Xuxuan Xie,Yifan Zhou,Ruizhe Chen,Zhiwang Zhou,Xingjian Guo,Yuhao Zhou,Xuming He,Shengyuan Xu,Xinyu Gu,Jiamin Wu,Mianxin Liu,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Dongzhan Zhou,Shixiang Tang,Yuqiang Li,Mao Su,Peng Ye,Siqi Sun,Bin Wang,Xue Yang,Zhenfei Yin,Tianfan Fu,Guangtao Zhai,Wanli Ouyang,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ResearchClawBench基准，用于评估AI智能体在10个科学领域40项任务中的自主科研能力。当前最强自主智能体(Claude Code)平均得分21.5，最强LLM(Claude-Opus-4.7)平均得分20.7，远未达到可靠复现目标论文的水平。错误主要集中于实验方案不匹配、证据不匹配和缺失科学核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体日益用于科研工作，但其端到端自主研究能力难以验证，缺乏可复现的评估基准。

Method: 构建跨10个科学领域的40项任务基准，每项任务基于已发表论文并提供相关文献与原始数据。采用专家策划的多模态评分标准，将目标科学成果分解为加权标准进行评估，支持目标论文级再发现评估同时保留新发现空间。

Result: 七种自主研究智能体中Claude Code表现最强(21.5分)，十七种原生LLM中Claude-Opus-4.7最强(20.7分)，LLM前沿均值仅26.5分，远未达到可靠复现水平。

Conclusion: ResearchClawBench为衡量自主科学研究的进展提供了可复现的评估前沿，当前系统距离可靠再发现仍有显著差距。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly used for scientific work, but their end-to-end autonomous research capability remains difficult to verify. We present ResearchClawBench, a benchmark for evaluating autonomous scientific research across 40 tasks from 10 scientific domains. Each task is grounded in a real published paper, provides related literature and raw data, and hides the target paper during evaluation. Expert-curated multimodal rubrics decompose the target scientific artifacts into weighted criteria, enabling evaluation of target-paper-level re-discovery while leaving room for new discovery. We evaluate seven autonomous research (auto-research) agents under a unified protocol and seventeen native LLMs through the lightweight ResearchHarness. Current systems remain far from reliable re-discovery: the strongest autonomous agent, Claude Code, averages 21.5, and the strongest ResearchHarness LLM, Claude-Opus-4.7, averages 20.7, with an LLM frontier mean of only 26.5. Error analysis shows that failures concentrate in experimental protocol mismatch, evidence mismatch, and missing scientific core. ResearchClawBench provides a reproducible evaluation frontier for measuring progress toward autonomous scientific research.

</details>


### [126] [UNIQ: Conformal Calibration for Adaptive Conservatism in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07592)
*Aditya Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UNIQ提出一种通过共形预测实现状态自适应保守性的离线强化学习方法，在保持接近IQL内存开销的同时提升性能，尤其适用于数据覆盖不均的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL方法对所有状态施加统一的保守惩罚，忽略了不同区域数据覆盖程度的差异，导致在数据充足区域过度保守、在稀疏区域不够保守的问题。

Method: 基于IQL框架，训练多分位点值集成，利用分割共形预测计算无分布假设的不确定性估计，并将不确定性信号映射为状态自适应的分位点，在数据覆盖好的区域放松保守性，在数据前沿附近的不确定区域加强保守性。

Result: 在D4RL MuJoCo基准上，UNIQ持续优于IQL，在Walker2d和replay-heavy任务上提升最明显；内存开销约250MB峰值VRAM，相比EDAC降低约10倍。

Conclusion: UNIQ并非追求整体SOTA性能，而是作为一种实用的机制贡献，改善离线强化学习中性能与效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning requires careful conservatism to mitigate distribution shift, yet most existing methods apply a fixed penalty uniformly across all states regardless of local data coverage. We present UNIQ (Uncertainty-Informed Quantile), an offline RL method that introduces state-adaptive conservatism through conformally calibrated uncertainty estimation. Built on the Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) backbone, UNIQ trains a multi-expectile value ensemble, computes distribution-free uncertainty estimates using split conformal prediction, and maps the resulting signal to a state-dependent expectile that relaxes conservatism in well-covered regions while strengthening it in uncertain regions near the data frontier. On D4RL MuJoCo benchmarks, UNIQ consistently improves over IQL, with the largest gains observed on Walker2d and replay-heavy tasks. At the same time, UNIQ operates at near-IQL memory cost (approximately 250 MB peak VRAM), providing roughly a 10x reduction compared to EDAC. Rather than pursuing overall state-of-the-art performance, we position UNIQ as a practical mechanism contribution that improves the performance-efficiency trade-off in offline reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [127] [Shortcuts in the Tail: Debiasing via Post-Hoc Spectral Compression of Fine-Tuning Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07596)
*Edward Sun,Dmitrii Troitskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对微调权重差ΔW进行SVD截断尾部奇异值，可以在不损失任务精度的情况下减少模型对虚假关联的依赖，显著缩小模型在少数群体上的表现差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调常引入虚假关联导致模型在少数群体上系统性失败，现有缓解方法需要重新训练、群体标签或反事实数据，缺乏简单有效的后处理方案。

Method: 对微调前后权重差ΔW进行SVD分解，截断排名靠后的奇异值（top-k截断），仅保留主要奇异分量，作为模型参数的后处理干预。

Result: 在3个指令微调模型（0.5B-7B）和4个分类基准上，该方法在<2%精度损失下缩小了所有细分群体的表现差距，在CivilComments上差距缩小高达5倍。

Conclusion: 虚假关联信息编码在ΔW的尾部奇异值中，SVD奇异基为研究微调学习内容提供了有用的坐标系；截断的缓解效果并非源于低秩近似或秩约束训练。

Abstract: Fine-tuning often introduces spurious correlations alongside task knowledge, causing systematic failures on underrepresented groups. Existing mitigations require retraining, group labels, or curated counterfactual data. We show a simple post-hoc intervention reduces shortcut reliance without any of these: truncating the tail of the SVD of $ΔW = W_\mathrm{ft} - W_\mathrm{base}$ reduces the spurious-group gap while preserving task accuracy. Across three instruction-tuned models ($0.5$B--$7$B) and four classification benchmarks, top-$k$ truncation reduces the gap on every cell at $<2$ pp accuracy loss, by up to $5\times$ on CivilComments. We propose this works because the shortcut response sits in the tail of the singular ordering of $ΔW$, a claim about how truncation behaves rather than about the raw singular values, which are broadly distributed and look the same across all four datasets. A controlled boundary case in which fine-tuning has only a shortcut to learn shows the predicted FT-to-base collapse, and bottom-/random-$k$ and matched-rank LoRA controls rule out generic low-rank approximation and rank-constrained training as the explanation. We read this as preliminary evidence that the singular basis of $ΔW$ is a useful coordinate system for studying what fine-tuning has learned.

</details>


### [128] [Repetition Mismatch: Why Data Mixture Experiments Don't Scale and How to Fix Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07597)
*Kevin Zhou,Lisa Alazraki,Kris Cao,Marek Rei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练数据混合比例在小规模实验中难以外推到大训练预算的根本原因是重复率不匹配：高质量小数据集的重复率随训练预算增长而变化，导致最优混合比例偏移。提出重复率控制采样方法，在仅使用1/16目标token的单次实验中即可将混合比例误差控制在0.05以内，远优于无控制情况下的0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练数据混合比例通常通过小规模实验调优并外推到大训练预算，但当高质量数据稀缺需重复使用时，这种外推经常失败，其根本原因尚未被隔离分析。

Method: 提出重复率控制（repetition-controlled）的子采样程序，通过匹配目标重复率来消除重复率不匹配带来的偏移效应。在双数据源（有限高质量数据+网络爬取数据）和三数据源场景下进行实验验证。

Result: 双数据源：单次重复率控制实验（1/16目标token）恢复的最优混合比例误差仅0.05，而无控制时误差达0.75；达到相同精度需3-4个horizon，消耗44-94%目标token预算。三数据源：仅需两次重复率控制horizon即可恢复最优混合比例，优于需完整双数据源实验的基线。

Conclusion: 重复动力学（而非规模本身）决定小规模混合实验的泛化能力；数据重复应被视为混合优化的第一类变量，而非数据有限的附带副作用。

Abstract: Pre-training data mixtures are commonly tuned by running small-scale experiments and extrapolating to the target training budget. When high-quality data is scarce and must be repeated, this extrapolation frequently fails, but the source of the failure has not been isolated. We show that a primary culprit is a repetition mismatch: because high-quality datasets are small, their repetition rate changes as the training budget grows, shifting the optimal mixture in ways that small-scale proxy experiments do not anticipate. A subsampling procedure that matches the target repetition rate controls for this effect. In a two-source setting combining limited high-quality data with web crawl, a single repetition-controlled experiment using only 1/16 of the target tokens recovers a mixture within 0.05 of the optimum for a 757M parameter model, compared to an error of 0.75 without repetition control. Achieving comparable accuracy without repetition control requires three to four horizons, consuming 44 to 94% of the target token budget. With three data sources, the larger mixture space requires more than a single experiment to constrain, but the approach remains effective: at the 757M scale, just two repetition-controlled horizons recover the optimal mixture, outperforming baselines that instead require the full two-source experiments to construct. Our results reveal that repetition dynamics, not scale alone, shape whether small-scale mixture experiments generalize. More broadly, they suggest that data repetition deserves treatment as a first-class variable in mixture optimization, rather than an inconvenient side effect of limited data.

</details>


### [129] [A Topological Characterization of Graph Neural Networks via Stochastic Block Model Embeddings on the n-Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07598)
*Gopal Anantharaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个拓扑框架，通过将图神经网络（GNN）在随机块模型（SBM）上的映射投影到单位球面上，生成一个低维、与问题无关的"指纹"，用于比较训练好的GNN，并支持迁移学习候选检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏系统性的拓扑框架来比较不同训练的GNN模型，难以实现跨模型迁移学习候选检索。

Method: 基于图极限理论（Stochastic Block Models在图论信号空间的诱导）、Frieze-Kannan弱正则引理及其图论信号扩展、以及MPNN在割距离下的Lipschitz连续性，构造了一个保测映射将SBM区域映射到单位球面的不相交球冠上。

Result: 对于任意给定容差，训练的MPNN可通过有界复杂度的阶梯图论信号近似；构建的显式映射可生成低维指纹，支持可视化和最近邻搜索，无需重新训练即可实现迁移学习候选检索。

Conclusion: 该方法为GNN模型比较提供了新工具，同时指出了高维度量集中现象（对LLM级别嵌入的障碍），并提出五个未来研究方向。

Abstract: We propose a topological framework for comparing trained Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) by mapping the Stochastic Block Models (SBMs) induced on the graphon-signal space of a Message Passing Neural Network (MPNN) onto the unit $n$-sphere $\sphere^{n-1}\subset\R^n$. The construction rests on three classical pillars: the \emph{compactness} of the cut-distance graphon space $(\Wo,\cutdist)$ \citep{lovasz2006limits,lovasz2012large}, the Frieze--Kannan \emph{weak regularity lemma} together with its graphon-signal extension due to \citet{levie2023graphon}, and the Lipschitz continuity of MPNNs with respect to the cut-distance. We show that, for any prescribed tolerance $\varepsilon>0$, a trained MPNN $Φ$ acting on a sufficiently large graph factors (up to $\varepsilon$) through a step-graphon-signal of bounded complexity, and we construct an explicit measure-preserving map $Ψ_n\colon[0,1]\to\sphere^{n-1}$ that places the SBM regions on disjoint spherical caps. This produces a problem-agnostic, low-dimensional ``fingerprint'' of a trained GNN that is amenable to visual inspection and to nearest-neighbour search across model zoos, enabling \emph{transfer-learning candidate retrieval} without retraining. We discuss the obstruction posed by concentration of measure in high dimension -- a phenomenon directly relevant to LLM-scale embeddings. We close with five concrete future research directions: hyperbolic and Grassmannian alternatives to the spherical model, Gromov--Wasserstein distances on graphon-signals as an isometry-free alternative to the $n$-sphere map, an information-geometric (Fisher) reformulation of the SBM manifold, persistent-homology fingerprints of layer-wise embedding clouds, and a spectral-distance baseline derived from the graphon eigendecomposition.

</details>


### [130] [DiffoR: A Unified Continuous Generative Framework for Universal Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07599)
*Hongxu Ma,Lin Wang,Chenghou Jin,Han Zhou,Jie Zhang,Xiaoyu Yang,Chunjie Chen,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffOR，将序数回归（Ordinal Regression）重新定义为连续生成式序数回归任务，利用扩散模型通过迭代去噪恢复连续的序数值，并采用双解耦策略（多尺度增量聚合+动态去噪感知）来保持序数拓扑结构，在12个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序数回归方法（如朴素回归、基于离散化的分类和生成方法）受限于量化伪影和缺乏全局序数拓扑感知，无法捕捉序数数据中非平稳的语义过渡，且强制使用刚性边界划分。

Method: 提出DiffOR框架：将序数回归建模为连续生成式任务，使用扩散模型通过迭代去噪恢复连续序数值；设计双解耦策略——空间上通过多尺度增量聚合将目标分解为层次化连续增量，时间上通过动态去噪感知将去噪步长与特征频率同步，实现由粗到细的精炼。

Result: 在涵盖四个领域的12个基准数据集上，DiffOR始终优于现有最先进方法，验证了其作为通用序数回归解决方案的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著增强了表示能力和机制可解释性，建立了一个新的标准，有望成为通用序数回归的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Ordinal Regression (OR) aims to predict target values with inherent order, underpinning critical applications across diverse domains, from recommender systems to computer vision. Though having evolved from naive regression to discretization-based classification and generation, existing paradigms remain fundamentally constrained by quantization artifacts and the lack of global ordinal topological perception. These methods typically enforce rigid boundary delineations, failing to capture the non-stationary semantic transitions inherent to ordinal data. In this paper, we propose a novel paradigm where OR is formulated as a Continuous Generative Ordinal Regression task. Under the novel paradigm, we introduce DiffOR, a unified framework that leverages diffusion models to recover continuous ordinal values via iterative denoising, thereby enabling the dynamic learning of soft semantic transitions. To explicitly preserve ordinal topology, we devise a Dual-Decoupling Strategy: Spatially, Multi-scale Increment Aggregation decomposes targets into hierarchical continuous increments; Temporally, Dynamic Denoising Perception synchronizes denoising steps with feature frequencies, ensuring robust coarse-to-fine refinement. Theoretically, we show that the proposed method can significantly enhance both representation capability and mechanistic interpretability. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmarks across four domains validate DiffOR's consistent superiority over state-of-the-art methods, establishing a new standard that demonstrates strong potential as a general-purpose solution for universal ordinal regression.

</details>


### [131] [Reachability and asymptotics of Gaussian Transformer dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07600)
*Albert Alcalde,Zhengping Ji,Enrique Zuazua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Transformer中的高斯分布在传播过程中保持高斯性，将其简化为均值-协方差的有限维双线性控制系统，并揭示了与Riccati方程的联系。针对时变控制可实现任意目标高斯分布的精确可达，而时不变参数下则存在稳定性与爆破两种动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Transformer的数据传播建模为概率测度空间上的非线性控制系统，旨在从动力学角度理解Transformer的表达能力，并建立与经典控制理论（如Riccati方程）的桥梁。

Method: 采用平均场Transformer模型（含自注意力与仿射前馈层），证明高斯分布在流作用下保持高斯性；利用该不变性将无穷维权度测度动力学降维为均值-协方差的有限维双线性控制系统，并借助可达性分析与谱条件刻画动力学行为。

Result: 时变控制下，任意协方差矩阵同秩的目标高斯分布可在有限时间内精确可达；时不变参数下，给出渐近稳定（趋于正定平衡点）或有限时间爆破（协方差发散）的显式谱条件。数值实验验证了理论预测。 

Conclusion: Transformer中高斯分布的高斯不变性为理解其表达能力提供了新视角，将Transformer与经典控制理论中的Riccati方程联系起来，并为设计Transformer的动力学行为提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We formulate data propagation through the Transformer, the machine learning architecture powering large language models, as a nonlinear control system on the space of probability measures. For the mean-field Transformer model with self-attention and affine feed-forward layers, we prove that Gaussian distributions remain exactly Gaussian along the induced flow. This invariance reduces the infinite-dimensional measure dynamics to a finite-dimensional bilinear control system governing the evolution of the mean and covariance, reformulates the expressive capacity of Transformers as a reachability problem for prescribed Gaussian moments, and reveals a novel connection with Riccati-type equations from classical filtering and control.
  For time-varying controls, we prove exact finite-time reachability of any target Gaussian distribution whose covariance matrix has the same rank as the initial one, this rank constraint being an intrinsic invariant of the dynamics. For time-invariant parameters, we derive explicit spectral conditions leading either to asymptotic stability toward positive-definite equilibria or to finite-time blow-up of the covariance.
  Numerical experiments complement the theory by showing that practical Transformers with Gaussian inputs remain close to moment-matched Gaussian distributions through early and intermediate layers, while Transformers with prescribed attention matrices reproduce the predicted covariance regimes: bounded evolution in stabilizing configurations and blow-up in destabilizing ones.

</details>


### [132] [LFNO: Bridging Laplace and Fourier via Transient-Steady Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07601)
*Jeongun Ha,Sanga Yoon,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LFNO是一个融合拉普拉斯与傅里叶神经算子的统一框架，通过双分支架构显式分解瞬态与稳态成分，在9个基准测试中显著优于现有方法，尤其擅长处理瞬态主导的ODE系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子（如LNO、FNO）在处理同时包含瞬态和稳态动力学特征的复杂系统时存在局限性，缺乏统一框架来有效建模多时间尺度下的动力学行为。

Method: 提出LFNO（Laplace-Fourier Neural Operator），采用双分支架构，分别利用拉普拉斯算子和傅里叶算子的频谱优势，显式地将系统动力学分解为瞬态分量和稳态分量进行联合建模。

Result: 在9个基准（3个ODE系统：Duffing、Lorenz、Pendulum；6个PDE系统）上，LFNO在瞬态主导的ODE系统中显著超越现有算子，在PDE基准上与FNO性能相当且优于LNO，同时展现出更好的稳定性和物理可解释性。

Conclusion: LFNO提供了一个鲁棒且统一的框架，能够有效学习跨多个时间尺度的复杂动力学系统，在保持竞争力的同时增强了模型稳定性和物理可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce the Laplace-Fourier Neural Operator (LFNO), a unified framework for modeling dynamical systems across transient and steady-state regimes by integrating the spectral advantages of Laplace and Fourier Neural Operators. LFNO employs a dual-branch architecture that explicitly decomposes system dynamics into transient and steady-state components. We evaluate LFNO on nine benchmarks, including three ODE systems (Duffing, Lorenz, and Pendulum) and six PDE systems (Euler-Bernoulli beam, Heat, Reaction-diffusion, Brusselator, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes). LFNO significantly outperforms existing operators on ODE systems, where transient dynamics dominate, and consistently surpasses LNO while achieving performance competitive with FNO on PDE benchmarks. Furthermore, LFNO offers improved stability and physical interpretability through its component-wise decomposition. These results demonstrate that LFNO provides a robust and unified approach for learning complex dynamical systems across multiple temporal scales.

</details>


### [133] [Sample-Efficient Post-Training for LEGO Spatial-Physics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07602)
*Yuhuan Yuan,Zhouliang Yu,Minghao Liu,Weiyang Liu,Ge Lin Kan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现数据导致的物理欺骗现象（PhysHack），提出基于模型的数据选择方法及样本高效的强化学习方法PVPO，提升了乐高组装生成的物理可行性、语义一致性和几何对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的乐高组装生成方法存在数据导致的物理欺骗问题（PhysHack），即生成的组装结构虽然满足物理约束，但存在几何错位、语义不一致或校准不良的问题，且物理有效性本身不足以作为可靠物理推理的代理指标。

Method: 提出基于模型的数据选择方法（仅使用少量训练数据），结合样本高效的强化学习方法PVPO，将物理可行性与体素空间几何奖励相耦合。

Result: PVPO在多种模型骨干和测试时缩放设置下，改善了结构和语义对齐、物理有效性、结构稳定性和校准性能，减少了后验拒绝采样的依赖，并通过校准结果缓解了PhysHack问题。

Conclusion: 物理有效性本身不足以保证可靠的物理推理，模型可能学会生成有效的结构但丢失语义或几何保真度；PVPO方法有效缓解了这一问题。

Abstract: LLM-based LEGO assembly generation requires both semantic grounding and physical feasibility. We identify a data-induced failure mode, PhysHack, in which the assemblies satisfy physical-validity constraints while producing structures that are geometrically misaligned, semantically inconsistent, or poorly calibrated. To address this challenge, we propose a model-based data selection approach that uses only a small fraction of the training data while improving physically grounded LEGO assembly generation. Building on the selected trajectories, we introduce PVPO, a sample-efficient reinforcement learning method that couples physical feasibility with voxel-space geometric rewards. Our results show that physical validity alone is an insufficient proxy for reliable physical reasoning: models can learn to generate valid structures without preserving semantic or geometric fidelity. Experiments across model backbones and test-time scaling settings demonstrate that PVPO improves structural and semantic alignment, physical validity, structural stability, and calibration, while reducing reliance on extensive post-hoc rejection sampling. In particular, results on calibration show that PVPO mitigates PhysHack by making test-time selection more predictive of semantic and structural quality.

</details>


### [134] [MetaEvo: A Meta-Optimization Framework for Experience-Driven Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07603)
*Bowen Ren,Heyan Huang,Yinghao Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaEvo框架，通过偏好优化和模块化架构使LLM智能体能够从任务经验中持续学习并提升推理能力，而非仅被动执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于经验的LLM智能体通常依赖记忆或启发式方法，但无法真正提升模型的学习能力，导致性能停滞和长期改进受限。

Method: 两阶段框架MetaEvo：先用偏好优化增强模型的原则抽象能力，再通过模块化智能体架构实现这些原则的积累与复用。

Result: 在多个推理基准上持续优于强基线，迭代过程中保持稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: 元优化能够有效使智能体从经验中学习，持续增强推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, yet most LLM-based agents are statically deployed and unable to improve through task interactions. Existing experience-driven methods often rely on memory or heuristics without enhancing the model's ability to learn, treating it as a passive executor and leading to early performance plateaus and limited long-term improvement. To address this issue, we propose MetaEvo, a two-stage framework for continual agent evolution that focuses on improving how the model learns from tasks experience, rather than solely on what it stores. MetaEvo first applies preference-based optimization to enhance the model's ability of principle abstraction, then enables the accumulation and reuse of these principles within a modular agent architecture. Experimental results on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that MetaEvo consistently outperforms strong baselines, maintains reliable improvement across iterations. These findings validate the effectiveness of meta-optimization in enabling agents to learn from experience and continually enhance their reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [135] [Contribution Weights: A Geometrical Analysis of Self-Attention Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07604)
*Harry Jake Cunningham,Nicola Muca Cirone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贡献权重（Contribution Weights）指标，通过考虑注意力权重、值向量幅度和方向对齐来更忠实衡量token重要性，并揭示注意力下沉（attention sinks）具有主动抑制信息的功能性角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于注意力权重的分析方法忽略了被聚合的值向量的几何属性（幅度和方向），导致对token重要性的衡量不准确。

Method: 提出贡献权重（Contribution Weights），一种基于投影的度量指标，综合了注意力权重、值向量幅度以及值向量与层输出的方向对齐程度。

Result: 贡献权重在识别语义关键token方面持续优于基于注意力的指标；揭示注意力下沉具有主动功能性角色，通过凸关系抑制信息、稳定表示。

Conclusion: 贡献权重是更忠实的token重要性度量工具，并成功揭示了注意力下沉的主动功能性作用，超越了以往认为其仅是被动存储库的观点。

Abstract: Analyzing attention weights has become a standard approach for interpreting the information flow of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, this approach has significant limitations as it neglects the geometric properties of the value vectors being aggregated. To address this gap, we introduce \emph{Contribution Weights}, a projection-based metric that quantifies a token's influence by accounting for it's attention weight, value magnitude, and directional alignment with the layer output. We demonstrate that contribution weights provide a more faithful measure of token importance, consistently outperforming attention-based metrics in identifying semantically critical tokens across different decoder-only models, tasks, and datasets. Further, our metric enables novel mechanistic analysis of \emph{attention sinks}. While previous work characterized sinks as passive repositories for excess attention, we reveal they serve an active functional role, suppressing information through a convex relationship between sink rate and output norm, stabilizing representations by opposing the semantic drift of low-confidence tokens.

</details>


### [136] [SRT: Super-Resolution for Time Series via Disentangled Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07605)
*Jufang Duan,Shenglong Xiao,Yuren Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SRT和SRT-large框架，利用解耦整流流、隐式神经表示和跨分辨率注意力机制，从低分辨率时间序列数据中重建高分辨率信号，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高时间分辨率的时间序列数据对精确分析至关重要，但获取成本高昂。现有图像超分辨率技术无法直接迁移到时间序列领域，亟需针对时间序列特性的超分辨率方法。

Method: SRT框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将输入分解为趋势和季节成分；2) 使用隐式神经表示对齐目标分辨率；3) 提出跨分辨率注意力机制引导高分辨率细节生成。SRT-large是在大规模预训练基础上的扩展版本。

Result: 在9个公开数据集上，SRT和SRT-large在多种尺度因子下均持续优于现有方法，且SRT-large展现出强大的零样本超分辨率能力。

Conclusion: SRT框架通过解耦整流流有效解决了时间序列超分辨率问题，各组件设计均得到有效性验证，为大范围时间序列分析提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained time series data with high temporal resolution is critical for accurate analytics across a wide range of applications. However, the acquisition of such data is often limited by cost and feasibility. This problem can be tackled by reconstructing high-resolution signals from low-resolution inputs based on specific priors, known as super-resolution. While extensively studied in computer vision, directly transferring image super-resolution techniques to time series is not trivial. To address this challenge at a fundamental level, we propose Super-Resolution for Time series (SRT), a novel framework that reconstructs temporal patterns lost in low-resolution inputs via disentangled rectified flow. SRT decomposes the input into trend and seasonal components, aligns them to the target resolution using an implicit neural representation, and leverages a novel cross-resolution attention mechanism to guide the generation of high-resolution details. We further introduce SRT-large, a scaled-up version with extensive pre-training, which enables strong zero-shot super-resolution capability. Extensive experiments on nine public datasets demonstrate that SRT and SRT-large consistently outperform existing methods across multiple scale factors, showing both robust performance and the effectiveness of each component in our architecture.

</details>


### [137] [QDSP: An Interpretable Structured Learning Framework for Predicting Death or Cerebral Palsy in Very Low Birth Weight Infants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07606)
*Ling Wang,Xiaolong Li,Hui Zhou,Jing Shi,Fuhao Zhang,Dapeng Chen,Nan Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QDSP框架，集成Quota引导的子空间采样和可微决策结构感知，用于极低出生体重婴儿出院时预后风险分层，在真实队列中准确率达0.92、AUC达0.9714，优于XGBoost等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极低出生体重婴儿出院时预后风险分层面临高维小样本临床数据挑战，现有方法缺乏可靠且可解释的预测工具。

Method: 提出QDSP框架，包含两个核心模块：QSS（基于Bootstrap的特征一致性估计构建稳定低冗余特征子空间）和DSP（使用可微软斜决策结构建模非线性临床交互并保留可追踪决策证据）。

Result: 在51例VLBWI队列上准确率0.9200、AUC 0.9714，优于XGBoost、TabNet、TabPFN；在三个外部公共数据集上保持竞争力；SHAP分析和决策路径追踪识别出cPVL和出生体重等关键临床预测因子。

Conclusion: QDSP为VLBWI出院时风险分层提供了可解释且稳健的框架，可支持新生儿重症监护中的早期个体化临床决策。

Abstract: Very low birth weight infants (VLBWI) are at high risk of mortality and severe neurodevelopmental impairment, including cerebral palsy, yet reliable discharge-time prognostic stratification remains challenging in high-dimensional and data-limited clinical settings. To address this problem, we propose QDSP, an interpretable structured learning framework that integrates Quota-guided Subspace Sampling (QSS) and Differentiable-decision-guided Structure Perception (DSP). The QSS module constructs stability-aware and low-redundancy feature subspaces through bootstrap-based feature consistency estimation, whereas the DSP module employs differentiable soft oblique decision structures to model nonlinear clinical interactions while preserving traceable decision evidence. The proposed framework was evaluated on a real-world VLBWI cohort comprising 51 infants and further validated on three public medical tabular datasets. On the primary cohort, QDSP achieved an accuracy of 0.9200 and an AUC of 0.9714, outperforming representative machine learning and deep tabular learning baselines, including XGBoost, TabNet, and TabPFN. Across external datasets, QDSP maintained competitive discrimination and calibration under varying sample sizes and clinical distributions. In addition, SHAP-based analyses and differentiable decision-path tracing identified clinically relevant predictors, including cystic periventricular leukomalacia (cPVL) and birth weight, consistent with established neonatal pathophysiological evidence. These results suggest that QDSP provides an interpretable and robust framework for discharge-time risk stratification in VLBWI and may support early individualized clinical decision-making in neonatal intensive care settings.

</details>


### [138] [Position: Genomic Model Research Must Move Beyond Anecdotal Evaluation of Interpretability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07607)
*Shasha Zhou,Mingyu Huang,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过转录因子结合位点预测的基准测试，揭示了当前基因组可解释机器学习（IML）方法存在严重缺陷：不同IML方法可能给出矛盾解释、无法定位已知调控基序、不能忠实反映模型内部决策。作者类比临床试验，提出分层验证框架，强调系统性评估一致性、忠实性和生物学有效性，而非仅靠" cherry-picked"的案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基因组IML研究过度依赖零星的、选择性报告的验证案例（anecdotal validation），缺乏系统性评估，导致结果不可靠。研究者需要更严格的验证标准来确保解释方法的可信度。

Method: 以转录因子结合预测为基准任务，对比多种IML方法在同一预测上的解释结果，评估其矛盾性、定位已知调控基序的能力，以及对模型内部决策过程的忠实程度。

Result: 发现了三个关键问题：(1)不同IML方法对同一预测给出矛盾解释；(2)无法准确定位已知的调控基序；(3)不能忠实反映模型内部决策过程。

Conclusion: 提出类似临床试验的分层验证框架，要求对基因组IML方法进行系统性评估，包括一致性、忠实性和生物学有效性的检验，终结仅靠"挑出看起来合理的案例"来验证的做法。

Abstract: Advances in machine learning and computational power have unlocked the predictive potential of the human genome, yet biologists now demand that these models also elucidate the underlying biological mechanisms. While interpretable machine learning (IML) techniques have been increasingly applied to bridge this gap, there has been a pervasive reliance on anecdotal validation: the vast majority of research relies on a single IML method and reports only isolated successful instances. Through a benchmarking study on transcription factor binding, we demonstrate the risks of current practices. We show that different IML methods can often (1) yield contradictory explanations for the same predictions, (2) fail to localize known regulatory motifs, and (3) fail to faithfully reflect the model's internal decision process. In light of this, we argue for a validation framework analogous to clinical trials: just as trials require rigorous design and adverse-event reporting, genomic interpretability must move beyond cherry-picked plausibility toward systematic assessment of consistency, faithfulness, and biological validity. To facilitate this, we propose a tiered framework to guide rigorous evaluation and reporting of genomic IML methods.

</details>


### [139] [LEAF: Growing Trees Without Branching for Speech-Aware Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07610)
*Argyrios Gerogiannis,Yekaterina Yegorova,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Venugopal V. Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEAF方法，通过回溯式树结构将GRPO的粗粒度token级奖励改进为span级细粒度信用分配，在语音任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO风格的语音大模型后训练方法将相同的终端奖励广播给所有token，忽略了语音条件下rollout批次中共享前缀的结构信息，导致信用分配粗粒度。

Method: LEAF：低秩探索自适应分叉。首先采样完整响应，选择高熵边界，按共享前缀对响应分组，利用后代奖励分配span级优势。无需在线分支或额外解码。

Result: 在语音问答和语音翻译基准上，相同rollout和LoRA预算下，LEAF优于GRPO；更小的LEAF模型超越当前全参数微调的最优基线。

Conclusion: LEAF通过回溯式树结构恢复响应内的决策结构，实现更精细的信用分配，验证了span级优势设计的有效性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art GRPO-style methods for speech-aware large language model post-training suffer from coarse credit assignment, broadcasting the same terminal-reward advantage to every token in a response. This ignores useful structure within rollout batches, where speech-conditioned completions often share prefixes before diverging at important decisions. We propose Low-rank Exploration with Adaptive Forking (LEAF), a retrospective tree-based RL method that recovers this structure without online branching or additional decoding. LEAF samples complete responses, selects high-surprisal boundaries, groups responses by shared prefixes, and assigns span-level advantages using descendant rewards. We theoretically justify LEAF's span-level credit assignment and boundary-selection design. Empirically, LEAF improves over GRPO across speech question answering and speech translation benchmarks under the same rollout and low-rank adaptation budget. Notably, smaller LEAF-trained models outperform current state-of-the-art, full-parameter baselines.

</details>


### [140] [Measuring Poverty and Inequality with Reduced Data: A Machine Learning Approach Using Nigerian Household Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07614)
*Vanesa Jordá,Miguel Niño-Zarazúa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用随机森林递归特征消除（RF-RFE）方法，基于2018/19年尼日利亚通用家庭调查-面板数据，探讨是否可以通过精简调查工具保留关键的福利分布信息。结果显示，使用少量预测变量即可准确预测贫困状态和不平等线位置，表明机器学习方法有助于优化调查设计、降低数据需求，同时保留衡量贫困与不平等所需的大部分分布信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低收入和中等收入国家可靠测量收入与消费对监测贫困和不平等至关重要，但全面家庭调查成本高昂且难以定期实施。因此，需要探索能否通过精简调查工具来保留关键的分布信息，以降低数据收集负担。

Method: 采用随机森林递归特征消除（RF-RFE）方法，应用于2018/19年尼日利亚通用家庭调查-面板数据，识别最能对福利分布中个体进行分类的收入来源、消费类别和家庭特征。分析聚焦三个结果：贫困状态、五分位分布位置以及相对基尼不平等线的位置，并在调查的种植后和收获后两个时期评估季节性表现。

Result: 消费方面，使用少量支出类别即可准确预测贫困状态和不平等线位置；五分位分类对季节性消费准确率约80%，年度消费（基于单次季节性访问预测）约60-65%。收入方面，贫困状态预测准确率约90%（五个预测变量），不平等线位置主要由劳动收入捕获。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法有助于改进调查设计、降低数据需求，同时保留衡量和监测贫困与不平等所需的大部分分布信息。

Abstract: Reliable measurement of income and consumption is essential for monitoring poverty and inequality in low- and middle-income countries, yet full household surveys are costly and difficult to implement regularly. This paper examines whether reduced survey instruments can preserve key distributional information. We apply Random Forest Recursive Feature Elimination (RF-RFE) to the 2018/19 Nigeria General Household Survey-Panel to identify the income sources, consumption categories and household characteristics that best classify individuals within the welfare distribution. The analysis focuses on three outcomes: poverty status, location in the quintile distribution and position relative to the Gini-based inequality line. The survey's post-planting and post-harvest periods allow us to assess performance under different seasonal contexts. Results show that RF-RFE achieves strong classification accuracy with few predictors. For consumption, poverty status and inequality-line position are accurately predicted using a small set of expenditure categories, while quintile classification reaches about 80 percent accuracy for seasonal consumption and 60--65 percent for annual consumption predicted from a single seasonal visit. For income, poverty status reaches around 90 percent accuracy with five predictors, and inequality-line position is largely captured by labour earnings. The findings suggest that machine-learning methods can help improve survey design and reduce data requirements while retaining much of the distributional information needed to measure and monitor poverty and inequality.

</details>


### [141] [Structured Neuron Pruning in Deep Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07615)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多臂老虎机（MAB）的结构化剪枝框架，通过将每个神经元视为一个"臂"，在训练过程中动态评估并移除冗余神经元，实验表明UCB1和Thompson Sampling策略在多个任务上表现优异，显著优于未剪枝模型及其他基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中存在大量冗余隐藏单元，传统权重剪枝虽能减少参数量，但非结构化稀疏性在标准密集实现中难以有效利用，因此需要一种能直接移除完整神经元的结构化剪枝方法。

Method: 提出基于多臂老虎机（MAB）的结构化剪枝框架，将每个候选神经元视为一个臂。拉动臂时临时屏蔽该神经元，在采样的小批量数据上测量损失变化，然后恢复神经元并更新其安全移除奖励的估计。支持随机策略（Epsilon-Greedy、Softmax、UCB1、Thompson Sampling）和乘性权重策略（Hedge风格乘性权重、EXP3）。

Result: 在表格分类任务中，UCB1获得最高平均秩次；在回归任务中，UCB1同样获得最高平均秩次，且在R²指标上与多个标准回归模型相当或更优；在深度学习任务中，UCB1和Thompson Sampling获得最强秩次，多个MAB策略显著优于未剪枝模型、基于幅度的神经元剪枝和贪婪激活变化剪枝。

Conclusion: 基于MAB的神经元剪枝是一种有效且计算实用的结构化模型缩减方法。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often contain redundant hidden units. Removing individual weights can reduce parameter count, but unstructured sparsity is not always easy to exploit in standard dense implementations. This paper develops a structured pruning framework in which complete neurons are removed using multi-armed bandit (MAB) algorithms. Each candidate neuron is treated as an arm; pulling an arm temporarily masks that neuron, measures the change in loss on a sampled mini-batch, restores the neuron, and updates an estimate of its safe-removal reward. The framework supports stochastic policies, including Epsilon-Greedy, Softmax, UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, and multiplicative-weight policies, including Hedge-style multiplicative weights and EXP3. We evaluate the method on tabular classification, tabular regression and deep neural-network benchmarks covering image, text and reasoning tasks. Statistical comparisons using the Friedman test followed by the Nemenyi post-hoc test show significant differences between methods. On tabular classification tasks, UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank among pruning policies and improves on the unpruned neural network. On regression tasks, UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank and is statistically competitive with, or superior to, several standard regression models according to R^2. On deep-learning tasks, UCB1 and Thompson Sampling obtain the strongest ranks, and several MAB policies significantly outperform the unpruned model, magnitude-based neuron pruning and greedy activation-variation pruning. The results show that MAB-based neuron pruning is an effective and computationally practical approach for structured model reduction.

</details>


### [142] [Item Response Scaling Laws: A Measurement Theory Approach for Efficient and Generalizable Neural Scaling Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07616)
*Sang Truong,Yuheng Tu,Rylan Schaeffer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Item Response Scaling Laws (IRSL)，将项目反应理论(IRT)融入缩放定律框架，通过分离模型能力与问题特征，将参数复杂度从O(M×N)降至O(M+N)，仅需50个问题即可实现与传统方法相当或更优的决策精度（问题量减少99.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统缩放定律的评估成本过高，需要在数千个检查点或数百万次推理样本上进行昂贵评估。

Method: 提出IRSL统一框架，将项目反应理论(IRT)集成到缩放定律中，通过Beta-IRT利用语言模型的经验概率响应（如预训练中的token概率和测试时的通过率）来捕捉更丰富信号，将参数复杂度从O(M×N)降至O(M+N)。

Result: 在预训练下游缩放（6,612个检查点、37,682个问题）和测试时缩放（12个模型、120个问题、每个问题最多2,500个样本）两个范式上验证，仅用50个问题（减少99.9%）即可获得可靠缩放估计，决策精度与传统方法相当或更优。估计的潜在模型能力具有泛化性。

Conclusion: IRSL能够大幅降低缩放定律评估成本，同时保持或提高决策准确性，并且估计的模型能力可跨基准迁移。

Abstract: Scaling laws provide a fundamental framework for understanding the performance of Language Models (LMs), yet deriving them requires prohibitively expensive evaluations across thousands of checkpoints or millions of inference samples. To address this, we introduce Item Response Scaling Laws (IRSL), a unified framework that integrates Item Response Theory (IRT) within the scaling law framework. Unlike traditional approaches that treat each model-benchmark pair in isolation, IRSL disentangles latent model ability from question characteristics, factorizing the scaling law estimation for $M$ models and $N$ questions to significantly reduce parameter complexity from $O(M \times N)$ to $O(M + N)$. We instantiate IRSL with Beta-IRT, which leverages the empirical probability responses of LMs -- such as token probabilities in pre-training and pass rates in test-time sampling -- to capture richer signals than binary responses. We validate our approach across two prevalent scaling paradigms: (1) pre-training downstream scaling, using 6,612 LM checkpoints and 37,682 questions from 10 benchmarks; and (2) test-time scaling, using 12 LMs and 120 questions from 4 benchmarks with up to 2,500 samples per question. Given a one-time calibration on existing model responses, IRSL yields more reliable scaling estimates using only 50 questions per benchmark (a 99.9\% reduction), achieving comparable or superior decision accuracy to traditional approaches. Furthermore, we show that the estimated latent model abilities are generalizable, enabling accurate performance forecasting across benchmarks that share the same measurement objective.

</details>


### [143] [Query Lens: Interpreting Sparse Key-Value Features with Indirect Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07617)
*Hwiyeong Lee,Ingyu Bang,Uiji Hwang,Hyelim Lim,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Query Lens方法，通过联合考虑编码器和解码器特征，并纳入模块间接效应，比Logit Lens更全面、忠实地解释稀疏特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器提供的特征比单个神经元更可解释，但可靠地表征这些特征仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出Query Lens方法，扩展Logit Lens：1）联合考虑编码器侧关键特征和解码器侧价值特征；2）纳入下游模块处理特征时产生的间接效应。

Result: Query Lens能为Logit Lens无法解释的特征生成连贯的token签名。

Conclusion: 提出子空间通道假说，认为下游模块通过层特定子空间读取特征。

Abstract: While sparse autoencoders provide features more interpretable than individual neurons, reliably characterizing them remains challenging. We propose Query Lens, which extends Logit Lens to enable more comprehensive and faithful interpretations of sparse features. By jointly considering encoder-side key features and decoder-side value features, we identify both the inputs that activate a feature and the outputs it promotes. We also account for indirect, module-mediated effects that arise when the feature is processed by downstream modules, going beyond the direct effect captured by Logit Lens. In experiments, we find that Query Lens yields coherent token signatures for features that remain uninterpretable under Logit Lens. Finally, we propose the Subspace Channel Hypothesis, suggesting that downstream modules read features through layer-specific subspaces.

</details>


### [144] [ScaleSweep: Accurate NVFP4 Post-Training Quantization of LLMs via Block Scale Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07618)
*Li Lin,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaleSweep方法，通过扫描可行的NVFP4块尺度候选并选择最小化目标函数的解，显著提升4位量化精度，在Llama和Qwen模型上保持全精度93%以上性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NVFP4量化主要依赖AbsMax初始化尺度，与最优解存在明显差距，需要更优的尺度初始化方法。

Method: 提出ScaleSweep，遍历可行块尺度候选并选择最小化目标函数的解；推导了MSE和WMSE下所需扫描范围的上下界，大幅缩小搜索空间且不丢失最优解。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen模型上，ScaleSweep持续优于现有初始化方法，在权重量化、激活量化、KV cache和查询状态的端到端量化中保持全精度93%以上性能。

Conclusion: ScaleSweep是一种简单高效的NVFP4尺度优化方法，能显著缩小与全精度之间的差距，且开销可忽略。

Abstract: NVFP4 is a recently introduced hardware-supported FP4 format that improves the fidelity of 4-bit quantization through fine-grained block scales. However, existing NVFP4 scale initialization methods still primarily rely on AbsMax initialization, which leaves a noticeable gap to the optimal solution. To address this, we propose ScaleSweep, a simple and efficient scale optimization method that sweeps over feasible block scale candidates and selects the candidate that minimizes a target objective. We further provide a theoretical analysis of NVFP4 quantization and derive both lower and upper bounds for the required sweep range under mean square error (MSE) and weighted mean square error (WMSE) between the original tensor and the quantized reconstructed tensor. The proposed bounds substantially reduce the sweep space while preserving the optimal candidate, enabling negligible overhead compared with the baseline quantization operators. Experiments on Llama and Qwen models demonstrate that ScaleSweep consistently improves quantization performance over existing initialization methods and further narrows the gap to full precision. In particular, under aggressive end-to-end quantization of weights, activations, KV cache, and query states, ScaleSweep preserves more than 93% of the full-precision performance.

</details>


### [145] [Graph Neural Networks for Predicting Solvability of Finite Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07619)
*Tal Weissblat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个图神经网络框架，通过有限群的图表示（如Cayley图）来分类群的可解性，仅利用结构图信息训练模型区分可解群与非可解群，并在训练集外的群上评估模型泛化能力，作为探索GNN能否从图表示中学习代数性质的初步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索图神经网络能否从有限群的图表示中学习代数性质（如可解性），研究代数结构与图几何表示之间的关系。

Method: 使用图神经网络框架，基于有限群的图表示（包括Cayley图），仅利用结构图信息训练模型，区分可解群与非可解群。

Result: 在训练数据集之外的群上评估模型性能，验证GNN能否泛化学习群论中的代数性质。

Conclusion: 本研究作为概念验证，结果表明GNN能够从图表示中学习有限群的代数性质，为代数结构与几何表示之间的关系提供新视角。

Abstract: We present a Graph Neural Network (GNN) framework for the classification of finite groups according to their solvability. Using graph representations associated with finite groups, including Cayley graphs (CG), the proposed model is trained to distinguish solvable and non-solvable groups using structural graph information alone. The framework is evaluated on groups outside the training dataset in order to investigate the extent to which GNNs can learn algebraic properties arising in group theory. More broadly, the present work explores the relationship between algebraic structure and graph-based geometric representations of finite groups. The present study is intended as a proof-of-concept investigation of whether GNNs can learn algebraic properties of finite groups from graph-based representations

</details>


### [146] [HASA: Subnet Allocation for Compute-Constrained Model-Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07621)
*Amir Hossein Shahdadian,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Mahdi Taheri,Samira Nazari,Christian Herglotz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HASA方法，根据客户端数据异质性动态分配子网宽度，在固定计算预算下提升联邦学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型异构联邦学习的子网分配策略主要基于设备资源约束，未考虑数据分布统计异质性，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出异质性感知子网分配（HASA），根据客户端本地训练数据计算异质性分数，据此分配子网宽度，并强制执行固定的规模加权计算预算。

Result: 在7个客户端的下一个词预测任务上，HASA将平均测试准确率从13.82%提升至14.32%，同时提升了最差客户端准确率；在跨域图像分类任务上验证了异质性分数与模型宽度需求的关联性。

Conclusion: 基于数据异质性感知的子网分配能有效提升联邦学习性能，但效果依赖于异质性分数能否准确反映客户端对模型宽度的实际需求。

Abstract: Edge services increasingly use federated learning to personalize on-device models while keeping sensitive data local. In practice, deployments must handle heterogeneity in both client resources and local data distributions. Model-heterogeneous federated learning lowers client cost by allowing each client to train a subnet of a shared supernet, but most subnet-allocation policies are driven by device constraints and do not explicitly account for statistical heterogeneity. This paper proposes Heterogeneity-Aware Subnet Allocation (HASA), a train-only rule that assigns subnet widths based on client heterogeneity scores computed from local training data while enforcing a fixed size-weighted compute budget. This design enables budget-matched comparisons with alternative allocation policies. On an article-title next-word prediction benchmark with seven clients, HASA improves unweighted mean client test accuracy over uniform allocation across 10 matched seeds, increasing mean client test accuracy from 13.82 percent to 14.32 percent, and improves worst-client accuracy on average. In a matched-budget comparison with representative partial-training baselines, HASA achieves the strongest worst-client and tail-client accuracy on this benchmark. A directionality ablation shows that assigning smaller subnets to more heterogeneous clients degrades both mean and tail performance. A cross-domain image-classification study further shows that the effectiveness of heterogeneity-aware allocation depends on how well the heterogeneity score reflects clients' need for additional model width.

</details>


### [147] [Airport Terminal Passenger Queue Forecasting for Departure Gates and Security Checkpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07622)
*Juhwan Lee,Seokbin Yoon,Keumjin Lee,Hojong Baik,Seyeon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的机场旅客排队预测框架，利用历史运营数据（排队长度、等待时间、旅客吞吐量）预测未来最多两小时的排队情况，支持机场主动式拥堵管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场航站楼中旅客需求随时间变化且不同设施使用模式各异，导致排队预测困难，需要一种能捕捉时间依赖性和设施间关联的预测方法来实现主动拥塞管理。

Method: 采用Transformer架构从历史排队长度、等待时间（登机口与安检点）及值机岛旅客吞吐量中学习时间依赖与设施间相关性，并将学到的表征映射到两个设施专用的MLP头部分别预测登机口和安检点的排队长度与等待时间。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法能够准确预测未来最多两小时的排队情况。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够为机场航站楼运营提供实时的主动式排队管理和人员调配决策支持。

Abstract: Accurate passenger queue forecasting in airport terminals is essential for efficient departure operations, as it enables proactive congestion management. However, time-varying passenger demand and heterogeneous facility usage across multiple departure facilities make forecasting challenging. In this work, we propose a passenger queue forecasting framework that learns historical passenger flow patterns from operational data. The proposed model employs a Transformer-based architecture to capture temporal dependencies and inter-facility correlations using past queue length and waiting time at departure gates and security checkpoints, together with passenger throughput at check-in islands. The learned representations are mapped to two facility-specific MLP heads to predict queue length and waiting time at departure gates and security checkpoints. Experimental results demonstrate accurate forecasts up to two hours ahead. The proposed approach offers practical real-time decision support for proactive queue management and staff reallocation in airport terminal operations.

</details>


### [148] [Finite Certificates for In-Context Determinacy and a Threshold Theory of Emergence in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07623)
*Faruk Alpay,Hamdi Alakkad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过用有限语义证书替代基准标签，建立了一个验证上下文条件下语言模型行为的模型论框架，核心解决了两个问题：有限确定性（上下文何时能确定答案）和阈值涌现（基准性能跃升是否反映真实语义转变）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型验证依赖基准测试标签，但标签无法揭示模型在上下文条件下的语义确定性，也无法区分性能提升是源于真实语义理解还是评估指标的伪像。

Method: 采用模型论框架，引入有限语义证书概念；针对有限域线性任务族，证明了精确行空间准则、残差假设计数公式及提取最小强制子上下文的NP完全性；提出反幻象定理和速率敏感跨越界来区分阈值涌现与指标不连续性；证明了置信泛函是布尔概率测度（Keisler测度），并给出其Stone空间表示的不变性。

Result: 在有限域线性任务族上给出了完整和查询局部辨识曲线；证明了提取最小强制子上下文是NP完全的；证明了反幻象定理分离了阈值化指标与语义置信；给出了速率敏感的跨越界；建立了置信泛函的测度论和拓扑学性质。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一套完整的语义验证演算工具，包括有限上下文证书、对分隔子击中集、查询教学维度、提示保留准则和尺度极限见证，为语言模型行为的语义验证奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a model-theoretic framework for verifying context-conditioned language-model behavior by replacing benchmark labels with finite semantic certificates. The first problem is finite determinacy: when do examples in a context force the answer to a query without changing model parameters? In finite-field linear task families, we prove an exact row-space criterion, compute the residual hypothesis count, derive full and query-local identification curves, and show that extracting a smallest forcing subcontext is NP-complete even for binary outputs. The second problem is threshold emergence: when does an apparent benchmark jump reflect a semantic transition rather than a discontinuity of the scoring map? We prove an anti-mirage theorem separating thresholded metrics from semantic confidence and give a rate-sensitive crossing bound for latent commitments becoming visible above threshold. The common semantic object is a confidence functional on definable events. We show that it is a Boolean probability measure, equivalently a Keisler measure on the relevant type space, whose measure-one formulas form a proper filter and whose Stone-space representation is invariant under definitional expansion. The resulting calculus provides finite context certificates, pair-separator hitting sets, query teaching dimension, prompt-preservation criteria, and scale-limit witnesses.
